Amazon top ten per hardware category define the shared catalogue (NVMe/SATA separately); cases use all eligible products in the Amazon top 100. Existing authored requirements supply exceptions where the top ten cannot fill a role. Stored exact supplier matches supply costs. Prices use matching complete-PC sold evidence, then component sold medians, then recorded full cost plus 10% profit on cost. Unknown buying postage defaults to £0; selling-build delivery defaults to £15. Recorded charges take precedence. These are suggestions, not published customer offers.
